# Unit Profile Coverage Report

Generated by `scripts/unit-profile-coverage-check.cjs`.

Generated detail; do not edit or read wholesale. Start with `plans/unit-profile-coverage/README.md` and Report Health, then inspect only the relevant rows.

SRD 5.2.1 is conceptually part of Classic, but it is stored separately because the SRD collection has Creative Commons SRD provenance and distribution policy. The Classic 2024 library is a derived view from the SRD collection plus the Classic non-SRD mechanics-only collection; mixed authored provenance is not a valid collection state.

## Metric Semantics

Coverage rows are denominator-specific gates, not weighted completion scores. Unit-scoped denominators count distinct Unit identities; profile-scoped denominators count profile records or per-Unit profile facts as stated. Prose length, deferred-mechanics text, and source-row narrative weight do not add hidden denominator weight.

| Metric | Planning question | Measure | Denominator |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Installed collection inventory count | How many Unit records did the checker discover in the configured installed coverage collections? | installed Unit records discovered from configured collections | n/a; no independent expected-inventory boundary exists |
| Authored Surface Unit catalog admission | How much authored Surface Unit-shaped content is admitted to an installed Unit collection? | authored Surface records whose source path is installed | all authored Surface Unit-shaped records discovered |
| Authored Surface executable catalog admission | How much authored Surface content with executable mechanics is admitted to an installed Unit collection? | authored Surface executable records whose source path is installed | authored Surface Unit-shaped records with executable mechanics |
| Installed Unit profile classification coverage | Does every installed Unit have exactly one supported, unsupported, widening, or assumption disposition? | installed Unit records with a profile disposition claim | installed Unit records discovered from configured collections |
| Supported executable Unit coverage | How much installed executable Unit pressure is mapped to supported mechanics profiles? | installed Units with supported-profile claims | installed Units with executable mechanics |
| QNT profile modeling coverage | Do supported rule profiles that require executable semantics have QNT model owners? | executable profile records with at least one QNT owner | profile records whose kind requires executable evidence |
| QNT proof coverage | Do supported executable profiles have proof evidence? | executable profile records with qnt-proof verification owners | profile records whose kind requires executable evidence |
| Runtime mapping coverage | Do supported executable profiles have production runtime owners? | executable profile records with runtime owners | profile records whose kind requires executable evidence |
| Runtime parity coverage | Do supported executable profiles have focused MBT or runtime-test parity evidence? | executable profile records with focused-mbt or runtime-test verification owners | profile records whose kind requires executable evidence |
| Rules-kernel profile join coverage | Do rules-kernel-admitted mechanics profiles point to rules-kernel semantic obligations? | rules-kernel-applicable profile records with at least one profile-obligation mapping | profile records with rules-kernel profile kinds and either QNT owners or explicit profile-obligation mappings |
| Rules-kernel covered profile coverage | Do rules-kernel-admitted mechanics profiles point to covered rules-kernel obligations? | rules-kernel-applicable profile records whose mapped obligations are all covered | profile records with rules-kernel profile kinds and either QNT owners or explicit profile-obligation mappings |
| Supported Unit rules-kernel chain coverage | Do supported Unit identities have every rules-kernel-admitted profile connected to covered rules-kernel obligations? | supported Unit ids whose rules-kernel-applicable profiles all map to covered obligations | installed Units with supported-profile claims and at least one rules-kernel-applicable profile |
| Deterministic admission/projection coverage | Which supported Unit identities have deterministic production catalog/support/projection evidence? | supported Unit ids with deterministic-admission-projection evidence | installed Units with supported-profile claims |
| Selected identity replay coverage | Which supported Unit identities have intentionally selected concrete identity replay evidence? | supported Unit ids in the replay denominator with selected-identity-replay evidence | installed Units with supported-profile claims, excluding whole-claim selected-identity not-applicable dispositions |
| Classic non-SRD expression gate | Did every installed Classic non-SRD mechanics-only Unit pass the public-expression gate? | installed Classic non-SRD records passing validation | installed Classic non-SRD records |

## Unit Group Denominator Audit

Background, feat, spell, and class-feature groups are counted from installed Unit identities and supported profile facts. The selected-identity replay denominator is scoped to Unit identities that need replay evidence; whole-claim non-applicable dispositions are excluded, while deferred-portion non-applicable dispositions remain visible in a separate bucket.

| Group | Installed Unit denominator | Executable Unit denominator | Supported-profile Units | Profile-subset Units | Unsupported/other Units | Profile fact denominator | Selected-identity replay witnesses/denominator | Selected-identity replay gaps | Deferred selected-identity non-applicable |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Background Units | 4 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 4 | 0 | 0/0 | 0 | 0 |
| Feat Units | 13 | 13 | 12 | 0 | 1 | 14 | 8/11 | 3 | 0 |
| Spell Units | 191 | 191 | 98 | 54 | 39 | 209 | 148/148 | 0 | 0 |
| Class-feature Units | 118 | 118 | 94 | 10 | 14 | 146 | 86/86 | 0 | 0 |

| Denominator | Rule |
| --- | --- |
| executableUnitDenominator | installed Unit records in the group whose authored mechanics are executable |
| installedUnitDenominator | installed Unit records in the group; each Unit identity counts once |
| profileFactDenominator | sum of profileIds on supported-profile and profile-subset-supported Unit claims; one Unit can carry multiple profile facts |
| selectedIdentityDeferredNonApplicableUnits | profile-subset-supported Unit identities whose deferred mechanics are outside selected-identity replay, while the supported runtime portion still has no selected-identity replay witness |
| selectedIdentityReplayDenominator | supported-profile and profile-subset-supported Unit identities in the group, excluding whole-claim selected-identity not-applicable dispositions; deferred-portion non-applicable rows remain visible in their own bucket |
| selectedIdentityReplayGapUnits | Unit identities in the selected-identity replay denominator with no replay witness and no whole-claim or deferred-portion selected-identity non-applicable disposition |

## Rules-Kernel Join

The Unit matrix owns authored-content breadth. `plans/rules-kernel-coverage/profile-obligations.jsonl` owns the reducer-semantic join from rules-kernel-admitted mechanics profiles to QNT-connected rules-kernel obligations. Projection-only Character Creation and Character Sheet profiles with no QNT owner and no explicit profile-obligation mapping remain covered by their profile runtime evidence rather than this rules-kernel chain.

| `sorcerer_metamagic` | profile-subset-supported | supported subset: Character Creation admits same-class Sorcerer level-2 progression, discovers the Metamagic choice container from the installed Surface feature, and finalizes exactly two unique selected Metamagic option ids as CharacterBuild option facts instead of retained option Unit refs; Character Creation projects Metamagic source facts from the retained Surface feature and Sorcerer class level: known-option count thresholds at Sorcerer levels 2, 10, and 17; unique known-option roster; option Sorcery Point costs; Empowered and Seeking stacking exceptions; the one-option-per-spell default; and the shared Font of Magic Sorcery Point resource link without duplicating the Sorcery Point pool; Character Creation consumes the Metamagic class-level replacement fact in the Sorcerer level-gain workflow, replacing exactly one currently known Metamagic option with one currently unknown option while preserving the known-option count, and derives Sorcerer level 10 and 17 option gains from the installed Surface choice-count thresholds without duplicating class progression or option roster state; Character Sheet parsing preserves the selected CharacterBuild Metamagic option facts and rejects stored Metamagic option selections that do not match the retained feature and Sorcerer-level option count; Character Battle projects selected Metamagic option facts into battle as typed effectKind, stackingMode, and Sorcery Point cost facts, links them to the shared Font of Magic point-pool resource, and hands battle point-pool spending back through Character Sheet resourceExpenditures without dispatching on option authored identity; The Metamagic Spell Invocation boundary admits selected typed effectKind facts from known options, enforces affordability against the shared Sorcery Point pool, enforces one option per spell before option-specific support, rejects unsupported second-option effects instead of charging inert Metamagic, and promotes Quickened Spell for supported direct Hit Point restoration, scalar buff, save-gated damage, save-gated condition, save-gated condition-immunity, direct condition, roll modifier, direct spell-attack damage, independent spell-attack sequence, and creature size-change action-casting Spell Invocation shapes by discovering and resolving a Bonus Action subject that spends the Bonus Action, Sorcery Points, and any required Spell Slot while rejecting prior level-1-plus spell casts and committing the Quickened same-turn level-1-plus spell lock for later casts; The Metamagic Spell Invocation boundary promotes Careful Spell for supported action-time Saving Throw spell procedures by consuming selected protected-target facts, enforcing Charisma-modifier target limits and affected-target membership, spending 1 Sorcery Point from the shared Font of Magic point pool, making protected non-caster targets succeed on the relevant Saving Throw, suppressing successful-save half damage for protected targets, and preserving base successful-save no-effect behavior for save-gated non-damage procedures.; The Metamagic Spell Invocation boundary promotes Heightened Spell for supported action-time Saving Throw spell procedures by consuming one selected affected target fact, spending 2 Sorcery Points from the shared Font of Magic point pool, applying target-specific Disadvantage to that target's Saving Throw through the shared roll-mode projection, preserving ordinary save outcomes for all affected targets, rejecting non-affected selected targets before resource spending, and carrying the selected target through combatant-owned Hideous Laughter, occurrence-local Grease and Gust of Wind Line area, and combatant-owned repeating save-gated condition repeat-save lifecycles without duplicating spell occurrence state.; The Metamagic Spell Invocation boundary promotes Transmuted Spell for supported action-time spell damage procedures by consuming one selected replacement damage type from the RAW Acid, Cold, Fire, Lightning, Poison, and Thunder set, spending 1 Sorcery Point from the shared Font of Magic point pool, projecting the replacement through existing damage resolution facts, preserving authored spell identity and damage dice, and rejecting missing-replacement, non-listed, same-source-target, and unsupported-procedure selections before resource spending.; The Metamagic Spell Invocation boundary promotes Twinned Spell for Spell Slot casts whose Surface higher-slot targeting profile adds exactly one creature at the next effective spell level by projecting the next effective target maximum, spending 1 Sorcery Point from the shared Font of Magic point pool, preserving actual Spell Slot level and authored spell identity, and rejecting unsupported target-count shapes before resource spending.; The Metamagic Spell Invocation boundary promotes Distant Spell for promoted range-bearing target owners, currently Touch-range object-light targeting, by deriving a cast-local range fact from the selected Distant Spell and the canonical Surface range shape, spending 1 Sorcery Point from the shared Font of Magic point pool, consuming only range-bearing object target witnesses, preserving the base spell effect such as Light radii, and rejecting unsupported procedures, Self range, sub-5-foot distance ranges, missing range facts, and non-range-bearing fills before resource spending.; The Metamagic Spell Invocation boundary promotes Extended Spell for promoted duration-bearing spell owners, currently Enlarge/Reduce creature size change, by deriving a cast-local doubled duration fact from the selected Extended Spell and the canonical Surface duration shape, spending 1 Sorcery Point from the shared Font of Magic point pool, storing the doubled duration on the effect cleanup timer, projecting same-occurrence Concentration maintenance Saving Throw Advantage from the caster's Concentration state, and rejecting unsupported procedures, Instantaneous durations, and sub-1-minute durations before resource spending.; The Metamagic Spell Invocation boundary promotes Subtle Spell for action-time spell casts with component requirements by deriving a cast-local component projection from the canonical Surface component shape, spending 1 Sorcery Point from the shared Font of Magic point pool, suppressing Verbal and Somatic components, suppressing focus-replaceable Material components, preserving priced or consumed Material requirements, and rejecting non-action-time or no-component casts before resource spending.; The Metamagic Spell Invocation boundary promotes Seeking Spell for missed single spell-attack-damage procedures by reopening the pending Attack Roll fill with a typed Seeking Spell reroll option, enforcing known-option, shared Sorcery Point affordability, spell-attack-only, missed-original-roll, and different-Metamagic stacking gates before spending, applying the forced replacement d20 to hit, miss, natural-1 miss, and natural-20 critical resolution, spending 1 Sorcery Point only when the replacement roll is used, and preserving Ray of Frost's normal damage/effect flow without storing durable reroll opportunity state.; The Metamagic Spell Invocation boundary promotes Empowered Spell for pending single spell-attack-damage rolledDice fills, currently Ray of Frost at level 5, by reopening the damage roll with a typed damage dice reroll option, enforcing known-option, shared Sorcery Point affordability, original-die matching, Charisma-modifier minimum-one selected-die limits, and different-Metamagic stacking gates before spending, applying forced replacement dice before damage totals, spending 1 Sorcery Point only when replacement dice are used, and preserving the base spell damage/effect flow without storing durable reroll opportunity state.; deferred: Quickened Spell is supported for the promoted action-time Spell Invocation profiles named in supportedMechanics. Standalone damage, control, ongoing-effect, and other action-casting procedures outside those promoted profiles are explicitly outside the completed Metamagic subset until their own procedure owners consume typed Quickened resource-threading and parity witnesses. (closed: outside-battle-runtime); Distant Spell for additional range-bearing spell target or origin owners beyond the promoted object-light Touch-range slice, and Extended Spell for additional duration-bearing owners beyond the promoted Enlarge/Reduce creature-size slice. (closed: outside-battle-runtime); Empowered Spell for repeated spell attack sequences, Magic Missile allocation, Saving Throw area damage, ongoing damage, object damage, and other non-promoted spell damage owners remains deferred until each owner consumes a typed damage-reroll fill without duplicating damage roll state or violating one-use-per-spell accounting. (closed: outside-battle-runtime); Seeking Spell for repeated spell attack procedures such as Scorching Ray remains deferred until invocation-local one-use accounting can prevent more than one post-miss reroll per spell casting without durable reroll opportunity state.
